# Research to implement

**Date:** 2026-09-24
**Walk:** 95 of 95 slices from `docs/*.md`.
**Read:** 6611 names. **DROP:** 4619 (save keys, duplicates, games, desktop software — not listed).
**Unique KEEP sites:** 1992.

Build a row only when the action is **build**, or **hole** and you accept that cite. One folder, one verb, one key `ittYY-<slug>-lx`. Empty never writes. Leftover never writes the star. No invented logo. Stop at the cap. A list of slugs with no cite is not a build row.

| Year | Pass | Cap |
|---|---|---:|
| 2007 · 2010 · 2011 · 2012 · 2014 · 2016 · 2021 · 2022 | Double | 46 · 44 · 62 · 48 · 36 · 64 · 30 · 38 |
| 2013 · 2020 | Holes only | 54 · 38 |
| Other years | Stop | — |
